I haven't heard any voices or seen anything that isn't there. It's just the mood stuff, mostly.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where these feelings were so bad that you couldn't get out of bed?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, there are mornings where I really don't want to... where I just kind of want to stay under the covers and ignore everything. But I always manage to force myself up and log on for work. It's never gotten so bad that I actually couldn't get out of bed.\n\nDoctor: Would you say these low periods have been mild, moderate, or severe?\n\nPatient: I guess I would say they're... probably mild. I mean, since I can still function and get my work done every day, it doesn't feel severe or anything. It's mostly just a drag to get through, so yeah, I'd go with mild.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you've lost interest or pleasure in almost all activities most of the time?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I'd say so. Usually, I really look forward to the weekends—getting the grill going, maybe doing some fishing, or just putting on a record to relax. But lately, I just... I don't know, I just leave all that stuff sitting there. I just don't really care to do any of it right now.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way without any significant improvement?\n\nPatient: Well, looking back on it... I guess these low, guilty periods have been hanging around off and on for about eleven months now."
